title: "Encodian API reference"
sidebarTitle: "API"
description: "API reference for the Encodian plugin in Corsair: every `encodian.api.*` operation with its input and output types, taken from the plugin's Zod schemas."
---

<Info>
**New to Corsair?** See [API access](/concepts/api), [authentication](/concepts/auth), and [error handling](/concepts/error-handling).
</Info>

## Barcodes

### aiBarcodeRead

`barcodes.aiBarcodeRead`

Barcode - Read (AI)

**Risk:** `destructive`

```ts
await corsair.encodian.api.barcodes.aiBarcodeRead({"body":{"FileContent":"<base64-encoded PNG barcode file>"}});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `body` | `object` | Yes | — |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="body full type">

```ts
{
  FileContent?: string,
  startPage?: number,
  endPage?: number,
  barcodeRemoveControlChars?: boolean,
  cultureName?: string
}
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `barcodes` | `string[] \| null` | No | — |
| `HttpStatusCode` | `number \| null` | No | — |
| `HttpStatusMessage` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `OperationId` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `Errors` | `string[] \| null` | No | — |
| `OperationStatus` | `string \| null` | No | — |



---

### createBarcode

`barcodes.createBarcode`

Barcode - Create

**Risk:** `destructive`

```ts
await corsair.encodian.api.barcodes.createBarcode({"body":{"codeTextEncoding":"Default","dotCodemask":0}});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `body` | `object` | Yes | — |
| `query` | `object` | No | — |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="body full type">

```ts
{
  barcodeType?: AustraliaPost | Aztec | Codabar | CodablockF | Code11 | Code39Standard | Code39Extended | Code128 | Code16K | DatabarExpanded | DatabarExpandedStacked | DatabarLimited | DatabarOmniDirectional | DatabarStacked | DatabarStackedOmniDirectional | DatabarTruncated | DataMatrix | DotCode | EAN8 | EAN13 | GS1Code128 | GS1CodablockF | Interleaved2of5 | ISBN | ISMN | ISSN | ITF6 | ITF14 | MaxiCode | Pdf417 | Planet | Postnet | Standard2of5 | UPCA | UpcaGs1Code128Coupon | UpcaGs1DatabarCoupon | UPCE,
  barcodeData?: string,
  barcodeImageFormat?: PNG | JPG | SVG | BMP | TIFF | GIF,
  captionAbove?: string,
  captionBelow?: string,
  sizeMode?: None | Nearest | Fixed | Interpolation,
  height?: number,
  width?: number,
  backColor?: string,
  barHeight?: number,
  barColor?: string,
  filledBars?: boolean,
  borderColor?: string,
  borderStyle?: Dash | DashDot | DashDotDot | Dot | Solid,
  borderVisible?: boolean,
  borderWidth?: number,
  paddingBottom?: number,
  paddingTop?: number,
  paddingLeft?: number,
  paddingRight?: number,
  resolution?: number,
  rotationangle?: number,
  xDimension?: number,
  barcodeValueTextLocation?: None | Above | Below,
  barcodeCaptionAboveFont?: string,
  barcodeCaptionAboveFontSize?: number,
  barcodeCaptionAboveFontColour?: AliceBlue | AntiqueWhite | Aqua | Aquamarine | Azure | Beige | Bisque | Black | BlanchedAlmond | Blue | BlueViolet | Brown | BurlyWood | CadetBlue | Chartreuse | Chocolate | Coral | CornflowerBlue | Cornsilk | Crimson | Cyan | DarkBlue | DarkCyan | DarkGoldenrod | DarkGray | DarkGreen | DarkKhaki | DarkMagenta | DarkOliveGreen | DarkOrange | DarkOrchid | DarkRed | DarkSalmon | DarkSeaGreen | DarkSlateBlue | DarkSlateGray | DarkTurquoise | DarkViolet | DeepPink | DeepSkyBlue | DimGray | DodgerBlue | Firebrick | FloralWhite | ForestGreen | Fuchsia | Gainsboro | GhostWhite | Gold | Goldenrod | Gray | Green | GreenYellow | Honeydew | HotPink | IndianRed | Indigo | Ivory | Khaki | Lavender | LavenderBlush | LawnGreen | LemonChiffon | LightBlue | LightCoral | LightCyan | LightGoldenrodYellow | LightGray | LightGreen | LightPink | LightSalmon | LightSeaGreen | LightSkyBlue | LightSlateGray | LightSteelBlue | LightYellow | Lime | LimeGreen | Linen | Magenta | Maroon | MediumAquamarine | MediumBlue | MediumOrchid | MediumPurple | MediumSeaGreen | MediumSlateBlue | MediumSpringGreen | MediumTurquoise | MediumVioletRed | MidnightBlue | MintCream | MistyRose | Moccasin | NavajoWhite | Navy | OldLace | Olive | OliveDrab | Orange | OrangeRed | Orchid | PaleGoldenrod | PaleGreen | PaleTurquoise | PaleVioletRed | PapayaWhip | PeachPuff | Peru | Pink | Plum | PowderBlue | Purple | Red | RosyBrown | RoyalBlue | SaddleBrown | Salmon | SandyBrown | SeaGreen | SeaShell | Sienna | Silver | SkyBlue | SlateBlue | SlateGray | Snow | SpringGreen | SteelBlue | Tan | Teal | Thistle | Tomato | Transparent | Turquoise | Violet | Wheat | White | WhiteSmoke | Yellow | YellowGreen,
  barcodeCaptionAboveFontStyle?: Regular | Bold | Italic | Underline | Strikeout,
  barcodeCaptionAboveFontAlignment?: Left | Centre | Right,
  barcodeCaptionBelowFont?: string,
  barcodeCaptionBelowFontSize?: number,
  barcodeCaptionBelowFontColour?: AliceBlue | AntiqueWhite | Aqua | Aquamarine | Azure | Beige | Bisque | Black | BlanchedAlmond | Blue | BlueViolet | Brown | BurlyWood | CadetBlue | Chartreuse | Chocolate | Coral | CornflowerBlue | Cornsilk | Crimson | Cyan | DarkBlue | DarkCyan | DarkGoldenrod | DarkGray | DarkGreen | DarkKhaki | DarkMagenta | DarkOliveGreen | DarkOrange | DarkOrchid | DarkRed | DarkSalmon | DarkSeaGreen | DarkSlateBlue | DarkSlateGray | DarkTurquoise | DarkViolet | DeepPink | DeepSkyBlue | DimGray | DodgerBlue | Firebrick | FloralWhite | ForestGreen | Fuchsia | Gainsboro | GhostWhite | Gold | Goldenrod | Gray | Green | GreenYellow | Honeydew | HotPink | IndianRed | Indigo | Ivory | Khaki | Lavender | LavenderBlush | LawnGreen | LemonChiffon | LightBlue | LightCoral | LightCyan | LightGoldenrodYellow | LightGray | LightGreen | LightPink | LightSalmon | LightSeaGreen | LightSkyBlue | LightSlateGray | LightSteelBlue | LightYellow | Lime | LimeGreen | Linen | Magenta | Maroon | MediumAquamarine | MediumBlue | MediumOrchid | MediumPurple | MediumSeaGreen | MediumSlateBlue | MediumSpringGreen | MediumTurquoise | MediumVioletRed | MidnightBlue | MintCream | MistyRose | Moccasin | NavajoWhite | Navy | OldLace | Olive | OliveDrab | Orange | OrangeRed | Orchid | PaleGoldenrod | PaleGreen | PaleTurquoise | PaleVioletRed | PapayaWhip | PeachPuff | Peru | Pink | Plum | PowderBlue | Purple | Red | RosyBrown | RoyalBlue | SaddleBrown | Salmon | SandyBrown | SeaGreen | SeaShell | Sienna | Silver | SkyBlue | SlateBlue | SlateGray | Snow | SpringGreen | SteelBlue | Tan | Teal | Thistle | Tomato | Transparent | Turquoise | Violet | Wheat | White | WhiteSmoke | Yellow | YellowGreen,

  quietZoneCoef?: number,
  postalShortBarHeight?: number,
  wideNarrowRatio?: number,
  supplementData?: string,
  supplementSpace?: number,
  australianPostEncodingTable?: CTable | NTable | Other,
  australianPostShortBarHeight?: number,
  aztecErrorLevel?: number,
  aztecSymbolMode?: Auto | Compact | FullRange | Rune,
  codabarChecksumMode?: Mod10 | Mod16,
  codabarStartSymbol?: A | B | C | D,
  codabarStopSymbol?: A | B | C | D,
  code16KQuietZoneLeftCoef?: number,
  code16KQuietZoneRightCoef?: number,

  pdf417ErrorLevel?: Level0 | Level1 | Level2 | Level3 | Level4 | Level5 | Level6 | Level7 | Level8,
  pdf417MacroFileID?: number,
  pdf417MacroSegmentID?: number,
  pdf417MacroSegmentsCount?: number,
  pdf417Truncate?: boolean,
  codeTextEncoding: Default | Latin1 | BigEndianUnicode | UTF16 | UTF8 | UTF7 | ASCII,
  dotCodemask: number
}
```
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="query full type">

```ts
{
  barcodeTypeParameter?: AustraliaPost | Aztec | Codabar | CodablockF | Code11 | Code39Standard | Code39Extended | Code128 | Code16K | DatabarExpanded | DatabarExpandedStacked | DatabarLimited | DatabarOmniDirectional | DatabarStacked | DatabarStackedOmniDirectional | DatabarTruncated | DataMatrix | DotCode | EAN8 | EAN13 | GS1Code128 | GS1CodablockF | Interleaved2of5 | ISBN | ISMN | ISSN | ITF6 | ITF14 | MaxiCode | Pdf417 | Planet | Postnet | Standard2of5 | UPCA | UpcaGs1Code128Coupon | UpcaGs1DatabarCoupon | UPCE
}
```

</AccordionGroup>

### createQrCode

`barcodes.createQrCode`

QR Code - Create

**Risk:** `destructive`

```ts
await corsair.encodian.api.barcodes.createQrCode({"body":{"barcodeData":"x","barcodeImageFormat":"PNG"}});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `body` | `object` | Yes | — |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="body full type">

```ts
{
  barcodeData: string,
  barcodeImageFormat: PNG | JPG | SVG | BMP | TIFF | GIF,
  height?: number,
  width?: number,
  sizeMode?: None | Nearest | Fixed | Interpolation,
  foreColor?: string,
  backColor?: string,
  visibleBorder?: boolean,
  borderColor?: string,
  borderStyle?: Dash | DashDot | DashDotDot | Dot | Solid,
  borderWidth?: number,
  resolution?: number,
  encodeMode?: Auto | Bytes | ECIEncoding | ExtendedCodetext | Utf16BEBOM | Utf8BOM,
  encodeType?: Auto | ForceMicroQR | ForceQR,
  textEncodingType?: Default | Latin1 | BigEndianUnicode | UTF16 | UTF8 | UTF7 | ASCII,
  logoFileName?: string,
  logoFileContent?: string,
  logoPosition?: Top | Centre,
  captionAboveText?: string,
  captionAboveFont?: string,
  captionAboveFontSize?: number,
  captionAboveFontColour?: AliceBlue | AntiqueWhite | Aqua | Aquamarine | Azure | Beige | Bisque | Black | BlanchedAlmond | Blue | BlueViolet | Brown | BurlyWood | CadetBlue | Chartreuse | Chocolate | Coral | CornflowerBlue | Cornsilk | Crimson | Cyan | DarkBlue | DarkCyan | DarkGoldenrod | DarkGray | DarkGreen | DarkKhaki | DarkMagenta | DarkOliveGreen | DarkOrange | DarkOrchid | DarkRed | DarkSalmon | DarkSeaGreen | DarkSlateBlue | DarkSlateGray | DarkTurquoise | DarkViolet | DeepPink | DeepSkyBlue | DimGray | DodgerBlue | Firebrick | FloralWhite | ForestGreen | Fuchsia | Gainsboro | GhostWhite | Gold | Goldenrod | Gray | Green | GreenYellow | Honeydew | HotPink | IndianRed | Indigo | Ivory | Khaki | Lavender | LavenderBlush | LawnGreen | LemonChiffon | LightBlue | LightCoral | LightCyan | LightGoldenrodYellow | LightGray | LightGreen | LightPink | LightSalmon | LightSeaGreen | LightSkyBlue | LightSlateGray | LightSteelBlue | LightYellow | Lime | LimeGreen | Linen | Magenta | Maroon | MediumAquamarine | MediumBlue | MediumOrchid | MediumPurple | MediumSeaGreen | MediumSlateBlue | MediumSpringGreen | MediumTurquoise | MediumVioletRed | MidnightBlue | MintCream | MistyRose | Moccasin | NavajoWhite | Navy | OldLace | Olive | OliveDrab | Orange | OrangeRed | Orchid | PaleGoldenrod | PaleGreen | PaleTurquoise | PaleVioletRed | PapayaWhip | PeachPuff | Peru | Pink | Plum | PowderBlue | Purple | Red | RosyBrown | RoyalBlue | SaddleBrown | Salmon | SandyBrown | SeaGreen | SeaShell | Sienna | Silver | SkyBlue | SlateBlue | SlateGray | Snow | SpringGreen | SteelBlue | Tan | Teal | Thistle | Tomato | Transparent | Turquoise | Violet | Wheat | White | WhiteSmoke | Yellow | YellowGreen,
  captionAboveAlignment?: Left | Centre | Right,
  captionAboveNoWrap?: boolean,
  captionBelowText?: string,
  captionBelowFont?: string,
  captionBelowFontSize?: number,
  captionBelowFontColour?: AliceBlue | AntiqueWhite | Aqua | Aquamarine | Azure | Beige | Bisque | Black | BlanchedAlmond | Blue | BlueViolet | Brown | BurlyWood | CadetBlue | Chartreuse | Chocolate | Coral | CornflowerBlue | Cornsilk | Crimson | Cyan | DarkBlue | DarkCyan | DarkGoldenrod | DarkGray | DarkGreen | DarkKhaki | DarkMagenta | DarkOliveGreen | DarkOrange | DarkOrchid | DarkRed | DarkSalmon | DarkSeaGreen | DarkSlateBlue | DarkSlateGray | DarkTurquoise | DarkViolet | DeepPink | DeepSkyBlue | DimGray | DodgerBlue | Firebrick | FloralWhite | ForestGreen | Fuchsia | Gainsboro | GhostWhite | Gold | Goldenrod | Gray | Green | GreenYellow | Honeydew | HotPink | IndianRed | Indigo | Ivory | Khaki | Lavender | LavenderBlush | LawnGreen | LemonChiffon | LightBlue | LightCoral | LightCyan | LightGoldenrodYellow | LightGray | LightGreen | LightPink | LightSalmon | LightSeaGreen | LightSkyBlue | LightSlateGray | LightSteelBlue | LightYellow | Lime | LimeGreen | Linen | Magenta | Maroon | MediumAquamarine | MediumBlue | MediumOrchid | MediumPurple | MediumSeaGreen | MediumSlateBlue | MediumSpringGreen | MediumTurquoise | MediumVioletRed | MidnightBlue | MintCream | MistyRose | Moccasin | NavajoWhite | Navy | OldLace | Olive | OliveDrab | Orange | OrangeRed | Orchid | PaleGoldenrod | PaleGreen | PaleTurquoise | PaleVioletRed | PapayaWhip | PeachPuff | Peru | Pink | Plum | PowderBlue | Purple | Red | RosyBrown | RoyalBlue | SaddleBrown | Salmon | SandyBrown | SeaGreen | SeaShell | Sienna | Silver | SkyBlue | SlateBlue | SlateGray | Snow | SpringGreen | SteelBlue | Tan | Teal | Thistle | Tomato | Transparent | Turquoise | Violet | Wheat | White | WhiteSmoke | Yellow | YellowGreen,
```

### createSwissQrCode

`barcodes.createSwissQrCode`

Swiss QR Code - Create

**Risk:** `destructive`

```ts
await corsair.encodian.api.barcodes.createSwissQrCode({"body":{"account":"x","amount":0,"creditor":{"countryCode":"Afghanistan","name":"x"},"currency":"CHF","debtor":{"countryCode":"Afghanistan","name":"x"},"imageFormat":"PNG","reference":"x"}});
```

### readBarcodeFromDocument

`barcodes.readBarcodeFromDocument`

Barcode - Read from Document

**Risk:** `destructive`

```ts
await corsair.encodian.api.barcodes.readBarcodeFromDocument({"body":{}});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `body` | `object` | Yes | — |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="body full type">

```ts
{
  fileType?: PDF | DOCX,
  FileContent?: string,
  barcodeReadConfidence?: Low | Medium | High,
  barcodeReadQuality?: Normal | High | Low | Max,
  startPage?: number,
  endPage?: number,
  barcodeRemoveControlChars?: boolean
}
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `barcodes` | `string[]` | Yes | — |
| `HttpStatusCode` | `number \| null` | No | — |
| `HttpStatusMessage` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `OperationId` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `Errors` | `string[] \| null` | No | — |
| `OperationStatus` | `string \| null` | No | — |



---

### readBarcodeFromImage

`barcodes.readBarcodeFromImage`

Barcode - Read from Image

**Risk:** `destructive`

```ts
await corsair.encodian.api.barcodes.readBarcodeFromImage({"body":{"barcodeImageFormat":"PNG","fileContent":"AA=="}});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `body` | `object` | Yes | — |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="body full type">

```ts
{
  fileContent: string,
  barcodeImageFormat: PNG | JPG | BMP | TIFF | GIF | EMF,
  barcodeReadConfidence?: Low | Medium | High,
  barcodeRemoveControlChars?: boolean
}
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `Value` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `HttpStatusCode` | `number \| null` | No | — |
| `HttpStatusMessage` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `OperationId` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `Errors` | `string[] \| null` | No | — |
| `OperationStatus` | `string \| null` | No | — |

### readQrCodeFromDocument

`barcodes.readQrCodeFromDocument`

QR Code - Read from Document

**Risk:** `destructive`
